INVARIANTS:
- Deterministic institute-scoped business truth; BASE/JV separation preserved.
- Booking/resource/history/checkout handoff is transactional; legacy finished bookings are not rebilled.
- Package balance, price coverage and immutable redemption history share one transaction and institute lock.
- Checkout customer correction uses the same lock; active coverage and paid states prevent beneficiary changes.
- Membership grant/renewal uses registered scoped identities, snapshotted terms and transactional validity/history; granting rights does not claim settled payment.
- Member pricing uses declared catalog prices and immutable application/reversal facts; active package/member coverage cannot stack.
- Checkout wallets share a scoped mutation lease; one wallet cannot unlock payment while the other remains unconfirmed.
- Gift currency is explicitly configured and frozen after issuance, loyalty policy or reward creation; credited amount/code/expiry are replay-safe. Granting credit does not claim a sale.
- New walk-in/booking checkout tickets snapshot configured currency; legacy unknown invoices require explicit unpaid confirmation. Paid history cannot be relabeled; catalog additions/member tariffs cannot mix current and ticket currencies.
- Gift/mixed closure requires recorded allocations and currency-matched immutable closure facts; mixed remainder requires an explicit method. Historical paid-ticket replay is preserved (ADR-006).
- Active gift tenders freeze price/beneficiary changes and cancellation until reversed; credit corrections preserve expiry. Service revenue is unchanged, and cashier confirmation does not claim external settlement.
- Minimal customer identities support selection; legacy references are preserved. Full Phase 12 CRM lifecycle and public self-service are not claimed.
- Loyalty balance derives from scoped immutable history; manager corrections are not automatic purchase earnings. Paid checkout awards points once from actual line totals using an explicit immutable rate/currency revision, rounded down to whole points; zero earnings are recorded. Failed earning writes roll back closure. Tier descriptions do not apply unconfigured discounts or infer loyalty from membership.
- Loyalty rewards debit points and reduce performed-line prices atomically; manager prepaid reversal credits the original cost and restores saved prices. Replays cannot undo newer coverage; active rewards block beneficiary changes/cancellation and history blocks line removal/reset.
- Entitlement issuance does not claim a paid package sale or external payment settlement.
- Demo reset cannot erase commercial redemption-linked tickets or durable payment requests.
- Active authorization executions freeze ticket mutations, commercial price changes and new gift funding/reversal; original intent recovery never re-quotes an unknown provider outcome. Pending/authorized outcomes retain the seal; explicit final failure releases it atomically with its immutable fact. Child cancellation releases the parent with its confirmed fact; captured funds remain protected until checkout application. Failed child requests allow a new operation while keeping history. No timeout-based release or external settlement is inferred.

PHASE10O_NATIVE_OUTLOOK_SCENARIOS:

Displayed known demand must also equal the original selected norm lines times supplied volumes; even arithmetically consistent altered demand is rejected. Initial tablet screenshot reviewed; scoped cards subsequently tightened repeated paragraph spacing. Both outlook cases use an America/Los_Angeles workstation and prove institute-midnight UTC boundaries, pinned norm quantities after a newer version, partial total-null coverage, unchanged stock/receipts and refusal of an arithmetically consistent altered reply. After adding norm-history read retry, final outlook-only cases: 2 passed with rebuild/cleanup, including an injected 503 followed by native refresh of the same selected service. Final canonical frontend suite: 136 passed, including six outlook cases, stale invalidated-read isolation, Zurich DST, Kathmandu minute offsets, skipped Apia date and ambiguous Havana midnight. Final tablet partial-scenario screenshot reviewed: readable fields/cards, 44px controls, explicit unknown total/conditional arrivals and no document overflow. No page exceptions were observed in the native cases.
- No backend/schema change. Phase 10N's 43 PostgreSQL-enabled relevant checks remain the API baseline. No scheduled-demand/observed-usage prediction, physical tablet, production migration or complete Phase 10/24 claim. NEXT: explicit scheduled/observed demand source and coverage contracts, then integration with native outlook.

PHASE10P_EXACT_PROJECTION_WINDOWS:
- Internal immutable projection window captures explicit UTC requested/effective future boundaries and historical boundaries. Future start clips expired time at the captured generation instant; expired, naive, nonpositive and over-366-day windows are refused. Observations use half-open actual-completion membership; a late consumption confirmation does not move the performed service into another period.
- Integer microsecond ratios and ceiling arithmetic preserve very large measured milli-quantities without floats or Decimal context dependence. ml/g round to one milli-unit; unit rounds to one whole article and refuses fractional measured articles. Zero is supported for explicitly measured zero; the primitive does not infer missing measurements or source coverage.
- Targeted projection/outlook regression: 18 passed with PostgreSQL opt-ins enabled, including 14 new primitive cases and the four existing exact outlook cases. Existing passlib crypt deprecation warning only. No schema/data mutation or frontend change. Prior native evidence remains Phase 10O (136 frontend checks, 26 combined Edge cases and two final outlook cases).
- ADR-016 records scope and integration requirements. This is an internal foundation, not a source-backed forecast endpoint. NEXT: scoped appointment/waiting-queue demand and completed-execution consumption aggregation, explicit incomplete coverage, then HTTP/native review. Phase 10, Phase 24 and production gates remain open.

PHASE10Q_RECORDED_UPCOMING_DEMAND_API:
- Read-only signed reception/manager POST `/inventory/institutes/{institute}/planned-demand` requires aware future boundaries and explicit strict boolean queue inclusion. It clips the elapsed part of the requested interval at generation, refuses ended horizons and returns bounded stock pages with source/count/coverage metadata. Shared item arithmetic preserves physical stock, pending orders and conditional shortage as separate facts.
- Actual unstarted scheduled/confirmed/arrived appointments contribute one service when their slot overlaps the remaining horizon. Finished/started/cancelled/no-show sources and the exclusive end boundary are excluded. Explicit queue inclusion counts actual waiting/assigned unremoved, unperformed line quantities; these remain unscheduled at read time, with no promised execution within the horizon. Foreign institutes do not contribute and customer identity is not returned.
- Current institute norm revisions are explicitly provisional at read time. Later approved revisions legitimately change the read. Missing norms leave complete item demand null across the page; explicitly disabled norms contribute zero. Missing/disabled service quantities are counted separately. Coverage describes recorded selected sources only, not unrecorded future arrivals or measured usage. No captured execution basis is backfilled and no stock/order/recipe mutation occurs.
- New PostgreSQL checks establish eight SELECTs independent of 250 added appointments, exact large-volume integer arithmetic under low Decimal precision and an actual pg_stat_activity Lock wait during a staged current-norm revision. The reader subsequently returns the committed revised norm. HTTP cases cover source/status/boundary exclusions, queue opt-out, missing/disabled coverage, revised norms, pagination, identity isolation and role/strict-input denial.
- Final relevant inventory/source/projection/migration bundle: 60 passed with PostgreSQL opt-ins enabled, including all three new planned-demand cases, 14 projection cases and the previous 43 relevant inventory checks. Existing passlib crypt deprecation warning only. Initial test fixture used nonexistent Institute.city_id and is corrected to required city; a missing-auth assertion expected 401 whereas the established route dependency returns 403 and is corrected. No schema/frontend change, full-backend, native source-forecast UI, physical tablet or production closure claim. NEXT: completed-execution observed consumption and explicit historical coverage, then native source-backed forecast review. Phase 10/24 and production gates remain open.

PHASE10R_SIGNED_OBSERVED_USAGE_API:
- Read-only signed reception/manager POST `/inventory/institutes/{institute}/observed-projection` requires aware historical/future intervals. Historical end must not exceed captured generation time; future time clips elapsed requested time. Completed sessions and appointments belong to the half-open period containing actual completion, never the potentially later stock confirmation. Only signatures dated no later than generation supply measured quantities. Current recipes and invoice removals do not substitute or remove signed measured usage.
- Response separates distinct completed executions, captured original bases, signed confirmations, pending bases and legacy completed sources without a basis. All institute undated completed sources are counted explicitly because their historical interval cannot be determined. Missing/pending/undated samples leave complete pace projection null; no dated completed sources are a distinct no-sample state. A signed empty measurement is explicit zero even for an unconfigured original norm. Multi-line walk-ins require every captured line's confirmation and remain one execution.
- Historical quantities and projected shortages use exact integers and elapsed microseconds. ml/g round upward to one milli-unit and whole articles to one unit. Repeated historical receipt/consumption cycles may aggregate above instantaneous stock capacity without precision loss. Constant-elapsed-time pace is an explicit assumption; complete sample coverage is not prediction confidence, and a subset projection is not a guaranteed future lower bound. Observed pace and recorded upcoming norms remain alternative views, never added together.
- Shared institute locking serializes measurements, source coverage and physical/procurement facts. Actual PostgreSQL lock wait across an atomic signed confirmation is observed; the reader sees committed 6.000 on hand, 4.125 measured and 2.063 projected together. Six SELECTs stay constant after 250 legacy completed sources are added. Global foreign sources do not enter the report. Invalid windows, roles/scopes and extra fields are refused; the query creates no stock/order/recipe/basis facts.
- Broader relevant inventory/source/projection/migration bundle: 64 passed with PostgreSQL opt-ins enabled. Final observed-only suite: 5 passed, including retained-invoice independence, multi-line confirmation coverage, historical capacity cycles, exact zero versus missing/no sample and actual PostgreSQL atomic confirmation/read locking. Existing passlib crypt deprecation warning only. The initial multi-line fixture omitted the start route's required service_id and is corrected. No schema/frontend change or new full-backend/native/physical/production claim. NEXT: native review of recorded upcoming demand and signed observed pace with explicit sample coverage. Phase 10/24 and production gates remain open.

PHASE10S_NATIVE_SOURCE_FORECAST_REVIEW:
- Inventory needs now offer Stock et scenario / Prestations et usage at the top, preserving each view's entered periods and original scenario choices. Inactive views hide facts and invalidate pending responses; returning re-reads the saved query before publishing. Reception/managers open read-only recorded upcoming sources or signed observed pace on explicit submission; staff/empty scope make no source query. No stock/order/recipe mutation is offered by these views.
- Institute civil-date boundaries, exclusive ends and the remaining effective future interval are explicit. Waiting queue inclusion is opt-in and unscheduled, with no guaranteed execution in the selected horizon. Upcoming norms are provisional. Source counts, missing/disabled norm quantities, original captured bases, signed/pending measurements, legacy executions and institute-wide undated completions are visible. Partial/no samples show unknown total projection; signed empty measurements remain distinct from missing facts. Upcoming demand and observed constant elapsed-time pace are alternatives, never added.
- DTO guards validate signed scope, page/horizon, exact microsecond clipping, count/coverage relationships, unit/quantity caps and threshold/order arithmetic. Observed pace is independently recomputed using integer microsecond ratios and whole-unit ceiling; recorded quantities also respect per-confirmation capacity. Large quantities avoid Number conversion. Parent stock invalidation, auth/timezone remounts, disposal and request sequences prevent old responses from reviving current facts.
- Initial frontend suite: 140 passed; final suite after adding sub-millisecond duration coverage: 141 passed (five new source cases). Production TypeScript/Vite builds pass. Initial native manual/source forecast cases: 4 passed. Combined signed Edge suite: 28 passed (14 desktop, 14 touch-enabled tablet-landscape). After the top view selector and checkbox layout improvements, final forecast-only cases: 4 passed with production rebuild and owned cleanup, including source and original scenario input preservation with fresh reads after changing views. America/Los_Angeles workstations use Europe/Zurich institute dates correctly. Historical fixtures are written only into the runner-owned marked temporary database, with original norm/execution/measured usage and a later provisional norm; no public test endpoint or real application database is changed. Native checks prove 8.250 upcoming norm / 24.750 with the actual two-unit queue line, original measured usage 4.125 after norm revision, exact elapsed-time projection, refusal/retry of an altered reply and unchanged physical stock. No browser page exceptions or document overflow are observed. Final tablet source cards/form screenshot inspected; readable scoped cards and 44px controls remain.
- No backend/schema change. Phase 10R's 64 relevant PostgreSQL-enabled checks and five final observed cases remain the API evidence. No new full-backend, physical tablet, statistical confidence, production migration or complete Phase 10/24 claim. NEXT: requirement/gate audit for inventory/reorder and remaining production/performance work, then independent canonical Workforce implementation.

PHASE10T_SCOPED_SOURCE_READS_AND_LOCAL_CALENDAR:
- Institute/status/completion and appointment-slot/queue-status compound indexes are declared in models and migration 2c897df4e6fb. No material/source/order/invoice facts are rewritten. Nine focused migration/query-plan cases pass with PostgreSQL enabled; a default PostgreSQL plan on 6,000 completed appointments uses the scoped completion index.
- A reproduced SQLite blocked-downgrade defect removed indexes while retaining purchase facts. Alembic's SQLite-only engine now owns BEGIN and transactional DDL; populated roundtrip and deeper refused downgrade preserve facts, schema and revision on both databases. Windows Python 3.13 SQLite roundtrip also passes. Runtime engines and PostgreSQL locking are unchanged. No existing application database migrated. ADR-017 records the decision and official SQLAlchemy guidance.
- First full PostgreSQL-enabled backend regression: 484 passed, one failed, no skips. The live action scenario exposed agenda UTC-day boundaries omitting an appointment from the institute's local day. Agenda/planning now use half-open local civil-day boundaries in UTC, including 23/25-hour DST days. Active overdue sessions remain visible. Signed agenda GET accepts an optional explicit civil day; no stored timestamps change. The live test reads its future booking's own institute day so crossing local midnight is valid. Six focused calendar/two-process cases pass; all four calendar cases also pass on Windows Python 3.13, including controlled extreme-date errors.
- Final full PostgreSQL-enabled backend regression: 489 passed, no failures or skips, in 1004.24 seconds. Existing passlib crypt and legacy SQLite datetime-adapter deprecation warnings remain. The final extreme-date guard is covered by four fresh Windows calendar cases; four fresh Linux calendar cases also pass after collection-time changes. Frontend source is unchanged; Phase 10S's 141 cases remain the component baseline.
- docs/architecture/INVENTORY_GATE_AUDIT.md maps every canonical Phase 10 requirement to implementation and evidence; all production/physical gates remain explicit. Production index rollout locks/duration, backup/restore, load SLOs and statistical forecast confidence remain unverified. Next independent substantive scope is Phase 11 Workforce, beginning with a bounded skill matrix from existing authoritative employee/service requirement facts.
- Calendar UI boundary: AppointmentPanel authoring/editing/clock labels and PlanningBoard axis still derive local civil time from the workstation. Existing native browser profiles use the institute zone and therefore do not establish cross-workstation agenda correctness. Fix this explicitly before relying on institute-local calendar UX; source-forecast and queue clock helpers already use institute zones.
- Final native compatibility check: four actual signed backend Edge cases pass (booking conflict/cancellation/retry and walk-in assignment/start/extension/finish/cash checkout on desktop and touch-enabled tablet-landscape), with production build and owned fixture cleanup. These profiles use Europe/Zurich; no cross-workstation authoring/axis claim is made.
- CHECKPOINT_STATUS = PASS at the controlled migration/API/browser scope above. Final calendar guard verification: 4 passed on Linux, 4 passed on Windows; no actual application database changed. Production and physical acceptance gates remain open. Backend explicitly declares tzdata for portable IANA timezone availability.

PHASE10U_NATIVE_INSTITUTE_CALENDAR:
- App now passes institute timezone to appointment authoring/editing, planning and availability detail. Civil dates, initial rounded slot, card/search clocks, edit fields, planning window/cursor and availability labels use the explicit institute zone, with existing UTC fallback. No host-zone interpretation of naive source instants.
- Wall-time resolution rejects invalid/skipped times and exposes an explicit first/second occurrence for repeated hours. Default/original actual instants retain their known occurrence; unchanged edit slots preserve the original start string including seconds/microseconds. Original replay keys survive failure within the same form. Institute/timezone/auth remounts prevent old local state from being reused.
- Planning uses elapsed instants between institute civil bounds. Repeated ticks show offsets and distinct positions; spring skips the absent hour. Cross-midnight clipping and actual durations across clock changes remain correct. One window is reused per board; explicit-zone formatter storage is bounded. The legacy 09:00-20:00/demo display is not a workforce hours policy.
- Native repeated-hour verification reproduced next-name erasure when authoring remained editable during the previous operation's refresh. Fields and simultaneous mutations are now guarded until completion. Authoring/occurrence controls have 44px minimum height. Tablet screenshot reviewed; occurrence choice is readable, with no document overflow.
- Final frontend suite: 151 passed (10 new calendar cases), including UTC/Los Angeles/Sydney hosts, Zurich spring/autumn transitions, Lord Howe half-hour transition, original precision/occurrence editing and pending-operation protection. Production TypeScript/Vite builds pass. Native initial booking/walk-in compatibility: 4 passed. Native repeated-hour/resource checks after pending-field fix: 4 passed. Broad signed backend Edge suite: 30 passed (15 desktop, 15 touch-enabled tablet-landscape) with owned cleanup; actual LA workstation booking creation/movement confirms correct institute clock, and two native future repeated-hour bookings are exactly one real hour apart, verified via signed explicit-day reads and cancelled. Final focused native resource/repeated-hour cases after 44px styling and action guard refinement: 4 passed with production rebuild and owned cleanup. Final tablet screenshot reviewed; availability detail and planning clock now agree in institute time.
- No backend/schema changes. Phase 10T's 489 PostgreSQL-enabled backend cases and final calendar guard checks remain the API baseline. No actual application database migrated, no physical tablet/production/performance/attendance certification claimed. Native future-day browsing remains unfinished and is the next coherent unit. ADR-018 records decisions and scope.
